Generic cubic-fourfold lattices, in numbered family order
H4 = H^4(X,Z), h^2 has square 3, and Lambda0 = (h^2)-perpendicular in H4.
T = Lambda0 fixed by the symplectic automorphism group; S = T-perpendicular in Lambda0.
A_gen = H4 intersect H^{2,2}(X) for a very general member of the family.
K = A_gen intersect Lambda0; P = K-perpendicular in Lambda0, and A_gen = P-perpendicular in H4.
For the general member of the containing connected symplectic family, P = T and K = S.
Thus S and T for a row are the K and P of that symplectic family, respectively.
Use the connected family, not merely the abstract isomorphism type of its symplectic group.
The displayed bases are those in the saved integral records.
q_L is the discriminant quadratic form on L^vee/L (values modulo 2Z).
Its OSCAR Conway-Sloane symbol lists only positive-scale Jordan blocks;
0 denotes the trivial discriminant form. Prime components are separated by spaces.
Symbols use the customary 3^{+1} notation for OSCAR's {3}^{1}.
